                CHALLENGE 2 UART CLI HELP MENU                
==============================================================
SYSTEM COMMANDS:
  help / menu / ?             - Display this menu
  dump_eeprom                 - Dump config database (protected by an exclusive key repeating over identity)
  get_mac                     - Output ESP32 eFuse hardware MAC address

CARD DATA COMMANDS (requires physical tap within 15 seconds):
  read_block [block]          - Read 16-byte block from card (e.g. read_block 4)
  write_block [block] [data]  - Write 16-byte block (hex characters)
                                Note: Inputs shorter than 32 hex chars
                                are automatically padded with zeroes.
                                Example: write_block 2 112233
  dump_card                   - Dump all blocks from card
  clear_card                  - Zero out all writable card data blocks
  provision_user_status       - Reset card session logs and roaming history without wiping entire card
==============================================================
